- **vcfops-api**: How to authenticate and interact with the VCF Operations Suite API. Use this skill whenever working with VCF Ops API calls, building API clients, debugging import/export failures, or planning which endpoint to use for a content management task. Also use when asked about VCF Operations, Aria Operations, or vRealize Operations API capabilities.
